oik_spanish_language_niger_delta_scripted_country_name = {
    country_trigger = {
        OR = {
            court_language = language:spanish_language
            culture.language = language:spanish_language
            overlord ?= {
                court_language = language:spanish_language
            }
            overlord ?= {
                culture.language = language:spanish_language
            }
        }
    }

    capital_trigger = {
        province_definition = province_definition:niger_delta_province
    }

    location_trigger = {
    }
}

    location_trigger = {

}
